1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the estimation structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 266. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 535 and converges at rate O(1/n^2).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the estimation space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 266 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 535. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any estimation module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 188. The projective resolution has length at most 140 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 188 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the estimation structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 140. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
